Filing description: MINUTE ENTRY for Trial Preparation Conference proceeding held before District Judge Gordon P Gallagher on 2/5/2024. Granting in part and denying in part 82 Motion to Strike. The motion is granted in part pertaining to Drs. Lonnie and Paul Zeltzer and they cannot testify regarding categories one through five. The motion is denied in part pertaining to expert witness Mr. Max Mancini, who is allowed to testify pertaining to his treatment and personal observations. [courtlistener] MINUTE ENTRY for Trial Preparation Conference proceeding held before District Judge Gordon P Gallagher on 2/5/2024. Granting in part and denying in part 82 Motion to Strike. The motion is granted in part pertaining to Drs. Lonnie and Paul Zeltzer and they cannot testify regarding categories one through five. The motion is denied in part pertaining to expert witness Mr. Max Mancini, who is allowed to testify pertaining to his treatment and personal observations. Mr. Mancini cannot testify regarding Post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD). Granting in part and denying in part 84 Motion in Limine. The motion is granted in part as Ms. Solondz may not testify as an expert witness under rule 702 pertaining to scientific, technical, or specialized knowledge and denied in part to allow Ms. Solondz to testify as a lay witness pertaining to her observations only under rule 701. Ms. Solondz may testify as a lay witness and parent regarding her personal experiences in caring for Plaintiff and personal observations of Plaintiffs mental and physical conditions but will not be permitted to base any conclusions regarding ailments or diagnoses based on her personal observations. the Motion in Limine [D. 84] is granted pertaining to Liatt Grannot and Dr.&lt;mark&gt;Leah Ellenberg&lt;/mark&gt;. The witnesses may only testify as lay witnesses. Video Trial Preparation Conference set for 2/23/2024 at 04:00 PM in Room 323 (Grand Junction). All parties are to submit all pleadings by February 20, 2024, to the Court for review. The Court will address Defendants amended proposed voir dire [D. 100] during the Trial Preparation Conference. At the time of the hearing, parties are to call 571-353-2301, access 435 004 390#. Court Reporter: Erin Valenti. (dclem) (Main Document 101 replaced on 2/5/2024) (dclem, ).
